### Problem 1172

- Source: https://www.erdosproblems.com/1172
- Status: open
- Prize: no
- Lean: https://github.com/google-deepmind/formal-conjectures
- OEIS: ["N/A"]
- Last edited: 23 January 2026

#### Statement (verbatim)

```text
Establish whether the following are true assuming the generalised continuum hypothesis:\[\omega_3 \to (\omega_2,\omega_1+2)^2,\]\[\omega_3\to (\omega_2+\omega_1,\omega_2+\omega)^2,\]\[\omega_2\to (\omega_1^{\omega+2}+2, \omega_1+2)^2.\]Establish whether the following is true assuming the continuum hypothesis:\[\omega_2\to (\omega_1+\omega)_2^2.\]
```

### Problem 1173

- Source: https://www.erdosproblems.com/1173
- Status: open
- Prize: no
- Lean: https://github.com/google-deepmind/formal-conjectures
- OEIS: ["N/A"]
- Last edited: 25 January 2026

#### Statement (verbatim)

```text
Assume the generalised continuum hypothesis. Let\[f: \omega_{\omega+1}\to [\omega_{\omega+1}]^{\leq \aleph_\omega}\]be a set mapping such that\[\lvert f(\alpha)\cap f(\beta)\rvert <\aleph_\omega\]for all $\alpha\neq \beta$. Does there exist a free set of cardinality $\aleph_{\omega+1}$?
```

### Problem 1174

- Source: https://www.erdosproblems.com/1174
- Status: not disprovable
- Prize: no
- Lean: https://github.com/google-deepmind/formal-conjectures
- OEIS: ["N/A"]

#### Statement (verbatim)

```text
Does there exist a graph $G$ with no $K_4$ such that every edge colouring of $G$ with countably many colours contains a monochromatic $K_3$?<br/><br/>Does there exist a graph $G$ with no $K_{\aleph_1}$ such that every edge colouring of $G$ with countably many colours contains a monochromatic $K_{\aleph_0}$?
```

### Problem 1175

- Source: https://www.erdosproblems.com/1175
- Status: open
- Prize: no
- Formalized: 1
- Lean: https://github.com/google-deepmind/formal-conjectures/blob/main/FormalConjectures/ErdosProblems/1175.lean
- OEIS: ["N/A"]

#### Statement (verbatim)

```text
Let $\kappa$ be an uncountable cardinal. Must there exist a cardinal $\lambda$ such that every graph with chromatic number $\lambda$ contains a triangle-free subgraph with chromatic number $\kappa$?
```

### Problem 1176

- Source: https://www.erdosproblems.com/1176
- Status: not disprovable
- Prize: no
- Formalized: 1
- Lean: https://github.com/google-deepmind/formal-conjectures/blob/main/FormalConjectures/ErdosProblems/1176.lean
- OEIS: ["N/A"]
- Last edited: 24 January 2026

#### Statement (verbatim)

```text
Let $G$ be a graph with chromatic number $\aleph_1$. Is it true that there is a colouring of the edges with $\aleph_1$ many colours such that, in any countable colouring of the vertices, there exists a vertex colour containing all edge colours?
```

### Problem 1177

- Source: https://www.erdosproblems.com/1177
- Status: open
- Prize: no
- Lean: https://github.com/google-deepmind/formal-conjectures
- OEIS: ["N/A"]
- Last edited: 23 January 2026

#### Statement (verbatim)

```text
Let $G$ be a finite $3$-uniform hypergraph, and let $F_G(\kappa)$ denote the collection of $3$-uniform hypergraphs with chromatic number $\kappa$ not containing $G$.<br/><br/>If $F_G(\aleph_1)$ is not empty then there exists $X\in F_G(\aleph_1)$ of cardinality at most $2^{2^{\aleph_0}}$.<br/><br/>If both $F_G(\aleph_1)$ and $F_H(\aleph_1)$ are non-empty then $F_G(\aleph_1)\cap F_H(\aleph_1)$ is non-empty.<br/><br/>If $\kappa,\lambda$ are uncountable cardinals and $F_G(\kappa)$ is non-empty then $F_G(\lambda)$ is non-empty.
```

### Problem 1178

- Source: https://www.erdosproblems.com/1178
- Status: open
- Prize: no
- Lean: https://github.com/google-deepmind/formal-conjectures
- OEIS: ["N/A"]
- Last edited: 26 January 2026

#### Statement (verbatim)

```text
For $r\geq 3$ let $d_r(e)$ be the minimal $d$ such that\[\mathrm{ex}_r(n,\mathcal{F})=o(n^2),\]where $\mathcal{F}$ is the family of $r$-uniform hypergraphs on $d$ vertices with $e$ edges.<br/><br/>Prove that\[d_r(e)=(r-2)e+3\]for all $r,e\geq 3$.
```

### Problem 1179

- Source: https://www.erdosproblems.com/1179
- Status: proved
- Prize: no
- Lean: https://github.com/google-deepmind/formal-conjectures
- OEIS: ["N/A"]
- Last edited: 26 January 2026

#### Statement (verbatim)

```text
Let $0<\epsilon<1$ and let $g_\epsilon(N)$ be the minimal $k$ such that if $G$ is an abelian group of size $N$ and $A\subseteq G$ is a uniformly random subset of size $k$, and\[F_A(g) = \#\left\{ S\subseteq A : g = \sum_{x\in S}x\right\},\]then, with probability $\to 1$ as $N\to \infty$,\[\left\lvert F_A(g)-\frac{2^k}{N}\right\rvert \leq \epsilon \frac{2^k}{N}\]for all $g\in G$.<br/><br/>Estimate $g_\epsilon(N)$ - in particular, is it true that for all $\epsilon>0$\[g_\epsilon(N)=(1+o_\epsilon(1))\log_2N?\]
```
